# Amazon Web Services (AWS) Documentation
> This file provides structured access to AWS service documentation for large language models. Each section represents a service landing page with links to key documentation resources.

## Guides



- [Amazon Linux 2 Release Notes](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AL2/latest/relnotes/relnotes-al2.md): Find the release notes for Amazon Linux 2, which provide information about new features, updates, and fixes. [llms.txt](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AL2/latest/relnotes/llms.txt)

- [AWS Resource Groups Resource Groups API Reference](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/ARG/latest/APIReference/Welcome.md): AWS Resource Groups lets you organize AWS resources such as Amazon Elastic Compute Cloud instances, Amazon Relational Database Service databases, and Amazon Simple Storage Service buckets into groups using criteria that you define as tags. [llms.txt](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/ARG/latest/APIReference/llms.txt)
